<script>
document.addEventListener('click',function(e){
 const btn=e.target.closest('.pp-copy-bib'); if(!btn)return;
 const bib=btn.getAttribute('data-bib');
 if(navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext){navigator.clipboard.writeText(bib).then(()=>{btn.textContent='Copied ✓';setTimeout(()=>btn.textContent='Copy BibTeX',1800)});}
 else {const area=document.createElement('textarea');area.value=bib;document.body.appendChild(area);area.select();document.execCommand('copy');area.remove();btn.textContent='Copied ✓';setTimeout(()=>btn.textContent='Copy BibTeX',1800)}
});
</script>
